efficiency.

We Are Hiring For 🚀 SAP ISU Tester 🚀 
Job Description: SAP ISU Tester
Job Title: SAP ISU Tester
 📅 Experience: 3+ years
 📍 Location: Hyderabad
Job Summary:
We are seeking an experienced SAP ISU Tester with a strong understanding of 
SAP ISU (Industry-Specific Utilities) testing processes. 
The ideal candidate will be responsible for ensuring the quality, 
performance, and reliability of SAP ISU modules, including Billing, Device 
Management, and Customer Service, through rigorous testing methodologies.
Key Responsibilities:
Test Planning & Strategy: Develop and execute test plans, test cases, and 
test scripts for SAP ISU modules.
Functional & Integration Testing: Perform functional, regression, system 
integration, and UAT (User Acceptance Testing) to ensure SAP ISU solutions 
work seamlessly.
Defect Management: Identify, document, and track defects using test 
management tools such as JIRA, HP ALM, or similar.
Automation Testing: Design and execute automated test scripts using tools 
like Selenium, UFT, or Tosca (preferred but not mandatory).
Performance Testing: Ensure the SAP ISU system performs efficiently under 
load using tools like LoadRunner or JMeter.
Collaboration: Work closely with SAP functional and technical teams, 
business analysts, and end users to resolve issues and ensure smooth 
deployments.
Test Data Management: Prepare and manage test data to support different 
testing phases.
Compliance & Documentation: Maintain proper documentation of test cases, 
test results, and defect reports.
Required Skills:
3+ years of experience in SAP ISU testing, covering Billing, Device 
Management, and Customer Service modules.
Hands-on experience in manual testing and automation testing of SAP ISU 
applications.
Strong knowledge of SAP ISU processes, including Billing, Metering, and 
Contract Management.
Experience with SAP ECC, S/4HANA (preferred).
Proficiency in test management tools like JIRA, HP ALM, or TestRail.
Good understanding of integration testing between SAP ISU and other SAP 
modules (SAP CRM, MM, SD, FICA).
Knowledge of automation testing tools like Selenium, UFT, Tosca (preferred 
but not mandatory).
Strong defect tracking and troubleshooting skills.
Excellent communication and stakeholder management skills.
Preferred Skills:
Experience in Agile/Scrum methodologies.
Exposure to performance testing tools (LoadRunner, JMeter).
SAP ISU Testing Certification (Preferred).
Experience with SAP Test Acceleration and Optimization (TAO).
Educational Qualification:
Bachelor's or Master’s degree in Computer Science, Information Technology, 
Engineering, or a related field.
